# Nepali grammar revised curriculum

## 1 हो: A is B (identity / equation)

Identify people and things

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use person/thing + identity noun + a form of हो. A noun names a person or thing, such as student or book. In म विद्यार्थी हुँ, म means I, विद्यार्थी means student, and हुँ agrees with I. Use छ for location or condition instead. Initial के can mark a yes/no question. Use the होइन family for negatives.

### Positive and negative forms for six basic person groups

| Person and reading | Positive and reading | Negative and reading |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I<br>म / ma | हुँ / hũ | होइनँ / hoinã |
| we<br>हामी / hāmī | हौं / haũ | होइनौं / hoinaũ |
| you<br>तपाईं / tapāĩ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| you plural<br>तपाईंहरू / tapāĩharū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| he/she<br>उहाँ / uhā̃ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| they<br>उहाँहरू / uhā̃harū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |

Use उहाँ for he/she and उहाँहरू for they as the basic forms. Both take respectful verb agreement without a masculine/feminine distinction. Familiar forms are taught in topic 7. Note the nasal first-person negatives होइनँ/छैनँ.

The ending changes with the subject. Identity statements use the होइन family for negatives, not the छैन family.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

I am a student.
म विद्यार्थी हुँ।
ma vidyārthī hũ

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| म | ma | I |
| विद्यार्थी | vidyārthī | student |
| हुँ | hũ | am; is; are |

How it works: Use person/thing + identity noun + a form of हो. A noun names a person or thing, such as student or book. In म विद्यार्थी हुँ, म means I, विद्यार्थी means student, and हुँ agrees with I. Use छ for location or condition instead. Initial के can mark a yes/no question. Use the होइन family for negatives.

| --- | --- | --- |
| तपाईं | tapāĩ | you (respectful) |
| विद्यार्थी | vidyārthī | student |
| हुनुहुन्छ | hunuhuncha | am; is; are |

How it works: Use person/thing + identity noun + a form of हो. A noun names a person or thing, such as student or book. In म विद्यार्थी हुँ, म means I, विद्यार्थी means student, and हुँ agrees with I. Use छ for location or condition instead. Initial के can mark a yes/no question. Use the होइन family for negatives.

## 2 छ: existence and location

Say what or who is present and where

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use person/thing + location + a form of छ. घरमा means at home; यहाँ, त्यहाँ and कहाँ mean here, there and where. Location + thing + छ introduces something present. Match a human subject in person and respect; use छन् for plural things. Possession with सँग + छ is taught in topic 5.

### Positive and negative forms for six basic person groups

| Person and reading | Positive and reading | Negative and reading |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I<br>म / ma | छु / chu | छैनँ / chainã |
| we<br>हामी / hāmī | छौं / chaũ | छैनौं / chainaũ |
| you<br>तपाईं / tapāĩ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| you plural<br>तपाईंहरू / tapāĩharū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| he/she<br>उहाँ / uhā̃ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| they<br>उहाँहरू / uhā̃harū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |

Use उहाँ for he/she and उहाँहरू for they as the basic forms. Both take respectful verb agreement without a masculine/feminine distinction. Familiar forms are taught in topic 7. Note the nasal first-person negatives होइनँ/छैनँ.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

I am at home.
म घरमा छु।
ma gharmā chu

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| म | ma | I |
| घरमा | gharmā | at home |
| छु | chu | is; are (existence) |

How it works: Use person/thing + location + a form of छ. घरमा means at home; यहाँ, त्यहाँ and कहाँ mean here, there and where. Location + thing + छ introduces something present. Match a human subject in person and respect; use छन् for plural things. Possession with सँग + छ is taught in topic 5.

This is a question. Supply the missing information and keep person agreement on the final verb.

## 3 छ: is ~ (adjective / state)

Describe qualities and current conditions

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use subject + adjective + a form of छ. Adjectives express qualities or conditions such as big, cold or busy. यो पानी चिसो छ means this water is cold. Use the छैन negative family, distinct from identity with होइन. खुसी and तयार describe being happy and ready. Weather expressions can also use nouns such as गर्मी.

With a person as subject, choose the appropriate form of छ. Do not confuse an adjective description with a noun identity such as being a doctor.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

This flower is beautiful.
यो फूल सुन्दर छ।
yo phūl sundar cha

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| यो | yo | this |
| फूल | phūl | flower |
| सुन्दर | sundar | beautiful |
| छ | cha | am; is; are |

How it works: Use subject + adjective + a form of छ. Adjectives express qualities or conditions such as big, cold or busy. यो पानी चिसो छ means this water is cold. Use the छैन negative family, distinct from identity with होइन. खुसी and तयार describe being happy and ready. Weather expressions can also use nouns such as गर्मी.

### Exercise 2

That book is old.
त्यो किताब पुरानो छ।
tyo kitāb purāno cha

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |

How it works: Use subject + adjective + a form of छ. Adjectives express qualities or conditions such as big, cold or busy. यो पानी चिसो छ means this water is cold. ## 4 हुन्छ: becomes / generally is (habitual, change)

Distinguish current state, regular situations and change

### Meaning and sentence construction

छ describes a current condition; हुन्छ can express a regular situation or what happens. आज चिसो छ means it is cold today; जाडोमा चिसो हुन्छ describes winter generally. The negative is हुँदैन. भयो expresses a past change or occurrence. बन्नु is used for becoming something such as a professional. Do not translate every हुन्छ as become.

### Six basic person groups with हुन्छ and its negatives

| Person | Positive and reading | Negative and reading |
| --- | --- | --- |
| म / ma | हुन्छु / hunchu | हुँदिनँ / hũdinã |
| हामी / hāmī | हुन्छौं / hunchaũ | हुँदैनौं / hũdainaũ |
| तपाईं / tapāĩ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| तपाईंहरू / tapāĩharū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| उहाँ / uhā̃ | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |
| उहाँहरू / uhā̃harū | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha | हुनुहुन्न / hunuhunna |

Being cold now and getting cold in winter take different perspectives. भयो is one form used for a change that has happened.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

The negative is हुँदैन. भयो expresses a past change or occurrence. बन्नु is used for becoming something such as a professional. Do not translate every हुन्छ as become.

## 5 Postpositions: place, participants and relationships

Mark place, participants and relationships after nouns

### Meaning and sentence construction

Postpositions follow nouns and express place, participants, starting points and other relationships. घर + मा gives घरमा, at home; घर + बाट gives घरबाट, from home. An English preposition does not always map to one Nepali form. The sections below teach meanings, combinations and short examples.

### Core forms

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| मा<br>mā | in, at, on (place/time) | घरमा / gharmā |
| लाई<br>lāī | recipient, marked object, experiencer | रामलाई / rāmlāī |
| को・का・की<br>ko / kā / kī | of; form agrees with the following noun | रामको किताब / rāmko kitāb |
| बाट<br>bāṭa | from; by a means of transport | बसबाट / basbāṭa |
| देखि<br>dekhi | from, since | बिहानदेखि / bihāndekhi |
| सम्म<br>samma | up to, until | पाँच बजेसम्म / pā̃c bajesamma |
| देखि…सम्म<br>dekhi…samma | from…to | सोमबारदेखि शुक्रबारसम्म / sombārdekhi śukrabārsamma |
| ले<br>le | with a tool; see 11 for agent marking | कलमले / kalamle |

### Company and possession

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| सँग・सित<br>sãga / sita | with, in company with | साथीसँग / sāthīsãga |
| को साथ<br>ko sāth | with; construction differs from attached सँग | साथीको साथ / sāthīko sāth |
| 人＋सँग＋物＋छ<br>person + sãga + thing + cha | possession: the thing, not the possessor, controls छ | मसँग कलम छ / masãga kalam cha |

### Direction and position

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| तिर・तर्फ・पट्टि<br>tira / tarpha / paṭṭi | towards, to the side; तिर also about a time | बजारतिर / bajārtira |
| माथि<br>māthi | above, on top; also above by itself | टेबलमाथि / ṭebalmāthi |
| तल・मुनि<br>tala / muni | below, under; distinguish standalone direction | टेबलमुनि / ṭebalmuni |
| भित्र・बाहिर<br>bhitra / bāhira | inside, outside | झोलाभित्र / jholābhitra |
| अगाडि・पछाडि<br>agāḍi / pachāḍi | in front of, behind | घरको अगाडि / gharko agāḍi |
| सामु・सामुन्ने<br>sāmu / sāmunne | in front of, before someone | मेरो सामु / mero sāmu |
| नजिक・निर・छेउ・छेउमा<br>najik / nira / cheu / cheumā | near, beside | घरको नजिक / gharko najik |
| बीच・बीचमा・बिच・बिचमा<br>bīc / bīcmā / bic / bicmā | between, in the middle; spelling variants | दुई घरको बीचमा / duī gharko bīcmā |
| वरिपरि<br>varipari | around | घरको वरिपरि / gharko varipari |
| पारि・वारि<br>pāri / vāri | on the far side, on this side | खोलापारि / kholāpāri |
| टाढा<br>ṭāḍhā | far; a reference point can use बाट | घरबाट टाढा / gharbāṭa ṭāḍhā |

### Extent

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| भर・भरि<br>bhara / bhari | throughout a place or period | दिनभरि / dinbhari |

### Further relationships

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| को बारेमा・सम्बन्धमा・विषयमा<br>ko bāremā / sambandhamā / viṣayamā | about, regarding | नेपालको बारेमा / nepālko bāremā |
| अनुसार<br>anusār | according to | नियमअनुसार / niyamanusār |
| बमोजिम・मुताबिक・अनुरूप<br>bamojim / mutābik / anurūp | in accordance with; further/formal reference | सम्झौताबमोजिम / samjhautābamojim |
| बिना・विना・बेगर<br>binā / vinā / begar | without; variants and synonyms | चिनीबिना / cinībinā |
| बाहेक<br>bāhek | except, apart from | आजबाहेक / ājabāhek |
| को सट्टा・को साटो<br>ko saṭṭā / ko sāṭo | instead of | चियाको सट्टा / ciyāko saṭṭā |
| प्रति<br>prati | towards in attitude or feeling | देशप्रति / deśprati |
| विरुद्ध・विपरीत<br>viruddha / viparīt | against, contrary to; context determines use | नियमविरुद्ध / niyamviruddha |
| मार्फत<br>mārphat | through, via; compare agent vs intermediary in 32 | साथीमार्फत / sāthīmārphat |

मसँग कलम छ literally places a pen with me and means I have a pen. The possessor being I does not change छ to छु. In साथीसँग जान्छु, सँग instead marks company.

को/की/का agree with the following noun. मेरो, हाम्रो and तपाईंको mean my, our and your. Pronouns can change before case markers: म→मलाई/मैले, उहाँ→उहाँलाई/उहाँले, उहाँहरू→उहाँहरूलाई/उहाँहरूले. Learn the form with its role.

Some pronouns change shape before a postposition. Distinguish म, मेरो, मलाई and मैले by their roles.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

I live in a house.
म घरमा बस्छु।
ma gharmā baschu

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| म | ma | I |
| घरमा | gharmā | at home |
| बस्छु | baschu | sit; live |

How it works: Postpositions follow nouns and express place, participants, starting points and other relationships. घर + मा gives घरमा, at home; घर + बाट gives घरबाट, from home. An English preposition does not always map to one Nepali form. The sections below teach meanings, combinations and short examples.

### Build your own sentence

Choose an example and reconstruct it from the translation. Change a noun, place or time and explain any required changes to the postposition or verb. Change the intended message, not only the subject.

### Review topics

2: छ: existence and location / 6: Question words and how to ask / 7: Pronouns, familiarity, respect and verb agreement / 11: The ergative ले & the past (did ~) / 25: Comparison & superlative / 26: Conjunctions (and / so / but) / 27: While ~ (-दै) / after ~ (-एपछि) / 28: In order to ~ (-नको लागि) / 32: Passive (be ~ed)

## 6 Question words and how to ask

Choose the right question word and answer it

### Meaning and sentence construction

A question word identifies missing information: who, what, where and so on. Decide what kind of answer you need and replace that part of a statement with a question word. Keep the final verb appropriate to person and tense. कुन asks for a selection, कस्तो a quality, कसरी a method and किन a reason. Distinguish initial yes/no के from what के.

### Core words and combinations with postpositions and counters

| Form and reading | Information requested |
| --- | --- |
| को<br>ko | who |
| के<br>ke | what |
| कहाँ<br>kahā̃ | where |
| कता・कतातिर<br>katā / katātira | which way, in which direction |
| कहिले<br>kahile | when |
| किन<br>kina | why |
| कसरी<br>kasarī | how, by what method |
| कति<br>kati | how many, how much |
| कुन<br>kun | which |
| कस्तो・कस्ती・कस्ता<br>kasto / kastī / kastā | what kind, what like |
| कत्रो・कत्री・कत्रा<br>katro / katrī / katrā | how big |
| कत्तिको・कति＋形容詞<br>kattiko / kati + adjective | to what extent |
| कसलाई<br>kaslāī | to whom, whom |
| कसले<br>kasle | who, as marked agent |
| कसको・कसकी・कसका<br>kasko / kaskī / kaskā | whose |
| कोसँग<br>kosãga | with whom |
| कसका लागि・कसको लागि<br>kaskā lāgi / kasko lāgi | for whom |
| कसबाट<br>kasbāṭa | from whom |
| कसको बारेमा<br>kasko bāremā | about whom |
| केमा・केबाट・केले<br>kemā / kebāṭa / kele | in what, from/by what, with what |
| केका लागि・केको<br>kekā lāgi / keko | for what, of what |
| कहाँबाट・कहाँसम्म・कहाँतिर<br>kahā̃bāṭa / kahā̃samma / kahā̃tira | from where, up to where, towards where |
| कहिलेदेखि・कहिलेसम्म<br>kahiledekhi / kahilesamma | since when, until when |
| कति वटा<br>kati vaṭā | how many objects |
| कति जना<br>kati janā | how many people |
| कति बजे・कति बज्यो<br>kati baje / kati bajyo | at what time, what time now |
| कति बेर・कति समय<br>kati ber / kati samay | how long |
| कति दिन・कति वर्ष<br>kati din / kati varṣa | how many days/years; also age |
| कति पटक<br>kati paṭak | how many times |
| कति टाढा<br>kati ṭāḍhā | how far away |
| कुनचाहिँ・कुन-कुन<br>kuncāhĩ / kun-kun | which one in particular, which ones |

कसलाई, कसले and कसको are case forms related to who; कोसँग means with whom. कति जना counts people, कति वटा objects, कति बजे asks clock time, and कति पटक counts occasions. कस्तो asks for a quality and कुन a selection; they are not interchangeable.

### Practice questions with their answers

### को

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | यहाँ को छ?<br>yahā̃ ko cha? | Who is here? |
| Answer | राम यहाँ छ।<br>rām yahā̃ cha | Ram is here. |

### के

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | यो के हो?<br>yo ke ho? | What is this? |
| Answer | यो किताब हो।<br>yo kitāb ho | This is a book. |

### कहाँ

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कहाँ बस्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kahā̃ basnuhuncha? | Where do you live? |
| Answer | म नेपालमा बस्छु।<br>ma nepālmā baschu | I live in Nepal. |

### कता・कतातिर

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कता जानुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ katā jānuhuncha? | Which way are you going? |
| Answer | म बजारतिर जान्छु।<br>ma bajārtira jānchu | I am going towards the market. |

### कहिले

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कहिले आउनुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kahile āunuhuncha? | When will you come? |
| Answer | म भोलि आउँछु।<br>ma bholi āũchu | I will come tomorrow. |

### किन

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं किन पर्खनुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kina parkhanuhuncha? | Why are you waiting? |
| Answer | म साथीलाई पर्खन्छु।<br>ma sāthīlāī parkhanchu | I am waiting for a friend. |

### कसरी

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कसरी जानुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kasarī jānuhuncha? | How will you go? |
| Answer | म बसबाट जान्छु।<br>ma basbāṭa jānchu | I will go by bus. |

### कति

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | यो कति पर्छ?<br>yo kati parcha? | How much does this cost? |
| Answer | यो सय रुपैयाँ पर्छ।<br>yo say rupaiyā̃ parcha | This costs one hundred rupees. |

### कुन

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कुन किताब पढ्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kun kitāb paḍhnuhuncha? | Which book will you read? |
| Answer | म यो किताब पढ्छु।<br>ma yo kitāb paḍhchu | I will read this book. |

### कस्तो・कस्ती・कस्ता

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | यो खाना कस्तो छ?<br>yo khānā kasto cha? | What is this food like? |
| Answer | यो पिरो छ।<br>yo piro cha | It is spicy. |

### कत्रो・कत्री・कत्रा

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईंको घर कत्रो छ?<br>tapāĩko ghar katro cha? | How big is your house? |
| Answer | मेरो घर सानो छ।<br>mero ghar sāno cha | My house is small. |

### कत्तिको・कति＋形容詞

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | यो काम कत्तिको गाह्रो छ?<br>yo kām kattiko gāhro cha? | How difficult is this work? |
| Answer | यो धेरै गाह्रो छैन।<br>yo dherai gāhro chaina | It is not very difficult. |

### कसलाई

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कसलाई फोन गर्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kaslāī phon garnuhuncha? | Who will you call? |
| Answer | म रामलाई फोन गर्छु।<br>ma rāmlāī phon garchu | I will call Ram. |

### कसले

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | कसले खाना पकायो?<br>kasle khānā pakāyo? | Who cooked the food? |
| Answer | रामले खाना पकायो।<br>rāmle khānā pakāyo | Ram cooked the food. |

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |

| Answer | यो पढ्नका लागि हो।<br>yo paḍhnakā lāgi ho | It is for reading. |

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |

| Answer | म बजारसम्म जान्छु।<br>ma bajārsamma jānchu | I will go as far as the market. |

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |

| Answer | पसल पाँच बजेसम्म खुला छ।<br>pasal pā̃c bajesamma khulā cha | It is open until five. |

### कति वटा

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईंसँग कति वटा कलम छन्?<br>tapāĩsãga kati vaṭā kalam chan? | How many pens do you have? |
| Answer | मसँग दुई वटा कलम छन्।<br>masãga duī vaṭā kalam chan | I have two pens. |

### कति जना

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | कति जना आउनुहुन्छ?<br>kati janā āunuhuncha? | How many people are coming? |
| Answer | दुई जना आउनुहुन्छ।<br>duī janā āunuhuncha | Two people are coming. |

### कति बजे・कति बज्यो

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | अहिले कति बज्यो?<br>ahile kati bajyo? | What time is it now? |
| Answer | अहिले पाँच बज्यो।<br>ahile pā̃c bajyo | It is five now. |

### कति बेर・कति समय

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कति बेर बस्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kati ber basnuhuncha? | How long will you stay? |
| Answer | म एक घण्टा बस्छु।<br>ma ek ghaṇṭā baschu | I will stay for one hour. |

### कति दिन・कति वर्ष

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कति दिन बस्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kati din basnuhuncha? | How many days will you stay? |
| Answer | म दुई दिन बस्छु।<br>ma duī din baschu | I will stay for two days. |

### कति पटक

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कति पटक आउनुभयो?<br>tapāĩ kati paṭak āunubhayo? | How many times have you come? |
| Answer | म दुई पटक आएँ।<br>ma duī paṭak āẽ | I came twice. |

### कति टाढा

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | पसल कति टाढा छ?<br>pasal kati ṭāḍhā cha? | How far away is the shop? |
| Answer | पसल दुई किलोमिटर टाढा छ।<br>pasal duī kilomīṭar ṭāḍhā cha | The shop is two kilometres away. |

### कुनचाहिँ・कुन-कुन

| Role | Nepali and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Question | तपाईं कुनचाहिँ लिनुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ kuncāhĩ linuhuncha? | Which one will you take? |
| Answer | म यो लिन्छु।<br>ma yo linchu | I will take this one. |

Compare कति बज्यो? (what time now) with कति बजे जानुहुन्छ? (at what time will you go). Age can be asked as तपाईंको उमेर कति वर्ष भयो? Forms such as भयो/आउनुभयो are past forms; see topic 11. Learn these natural constructions rather than translating word by word.

At the beginning of a sentence, के can mark a yes/no question rather than ask what an object is.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

What is this?
यो के हो?
yo ke ho?

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| यो | yo | this |
| के | ke | what |
| हो | ho | am; is; are |

How it works: A question word identifies missing information: who, what, where and so on. Decide what kind of answer you need and replace that part of a statement with a question word. Keep the final verb appropriate to person and tense. कुन asks for a selection, कस्तो a quality, कसरी a method and किन a reason. Distinguish initial yes/no के from what के.
This is a question. Supply the missing information and keep person agreement on the final verb.

### Build your own sentence

Choose an example and reconstruct it from the translation. Change a noun, place or time and explain any required changes to the postposition or verb. Change the intended message, not only the subject.

### Review topics

1: हो: A is B (identity / equation) / 2: छ: existence and location / 5: Postpositions: place, participants and relationships / 7: Pronouns, familiarity, respect and verb agreement / 11: The ergative ले & the past (did ~) / 23: Adjective agreement & position / 27: While ~ (-दै) / after ~ (-एपछि) / 28: In order to ~ (-नको लागि)

## 7 Pronouns, familiarity, respect and verb agreement

Choose pronouns and verbs for the social relationship

### Meaning and sentence construction

Ordinary practice prioritizes म, हामी, तपाईं, तपाईंहरू, उहाँ and उहाँहरू. तँ has low respect and can sound rude outside very close relationships. तिमी is familiar; तपाईं is respectful. Verb agreement changes with the form chosen. Familiar does not simply mean speaking to someone inferior.

### उ, उनी and उहाँ distinguish respect, not male versus female

| Pronoun and reading | Register | Examples with हो/छ/action |
| --- | --- | --- |
| उ / u | Low respect; can refer to either gender | हो / ho; छ / cha; गर्छ / garcha |
| उनी / unī | Medium respect; not female-only | हुन् / hun; छन् / chan; गर्छन् / garchan |
| उहाँ / uhā̃ | High respect; for either gender | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha; गर्नुहुन्छ / garnuhuncha |
| तँ / tã | Very close/informal; can be offensive | होस् / hos; छस् / chas; गर्छस् / garchas |
| तिमी / timī | Familiar, not simply for social inferiors | हौ / hau; छौ / chau; गर्छौ / garchau |
| तपाईं / tapāĩ | Respectful you | हुनुहुन्छ / hunuhuncha; गर्नुहुन्छ / garnuhuncha |

Explicitly feminine verbs include छिन्, गर्छिन् and गइन्. This does not make उनी a female-only pronoun. उनीहरू refers to they; उहाँहरू is respectful plural. तपाईंहरू is respectful plural you and retains respectful verb agreement.

| Base | To/object | Possessive/agent |
| --- | --- | --- |
| उ / u | उसलाई / uslāī | उसको / usko; उसले / usle |
| उनी / unī | उनलाई / unlāī | उनको / unko; उनले / unle |
| उनीहरू / unīharū | उनीहरूलाई / unīharūlāī | उनीहरूको / unīharūko; उनीहरूले / unīharūle |
| म / ma | मलाई / malāī | मेरो / mero; मैले / maile |
| हामी / hāmī | हामीलाई / hāmīlāī | हाम्रो / hāmro; हामीले / hāmīle |

उ and ऊ here represent spellings of the same low-respect third-person pronoun. आफू refers back as self; आफ्नो means own. Do not mechanically assign different pronouns to English he and she.

तँ is appropriate only in particular relationships. When unsure, use तपाईं and match it with a respectful verb form.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Review topics

1: हो: A is B (identity / equation) / 2: छ: existence and location / 5: Postpositions: place, participants and relationships / 8: Action verbs: present (do ~)

## 8 Action verbs: present (do ~)

Describe everyday actions and routines

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use subject + object/time/place + verb. Replace dictionary -नु with the appropriate present stem and personal ending. Learn stems through forms such as गर्नु→गर्छु, पढ्नु→पढ्छु and खानु→खान्छु. Adding every day or a time describes a routine.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म काम गर्छु।<br>ma kām garchu | I work. |
| we | हामी काम गर्छौं।<br>hāmī kām garchaũ | We work. |
| you | तपाईं काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩ kām garnuhuncha | You work. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩharū kām garnuhuncha | You work. |
| he/she | उहाँ काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃ kām garnuhuncha | She works. |
| they | उहाँहरू काम गर्छन्।<br>uhā̃harū kām garchan | They work. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Respectful forms follow a different pattern from ordinary endings. Remember to change the ending when you change the subject.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

This is a question. Supply the missing information and keep person agreement on the final verb.

## 9 Present negative (don't ~)

Describe actions you do not do

### Meaning and sentence construction

Action negatives use negative verb forms such as गर्दिनँ and खाँदिनँ, not an added छैन. Match person and respect: गर्छु→गर्दिनँ, गर्नुहुन्छ→गर्नुहुन्न, गर्छन्→गर्दैनन्. First-person forms have nasal endings; an explicitly feminine form can use गर्दिनन्.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म आज काम गर्दिनँ।<br>ma āja kām gardinã | I do not work today. |
| we | हामी आज काम गर्दैनौं।<br>hāmī āja kām gardainaũ | We do not work today. |
| you | तपाईं आज काम गर्नुहुन्न।<br>tapāĩ āja kām garnuhunna | You do not work today. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू आज काम गर्नुहुन्न।<br>tapāĩharū āja kām garnuhunna | You do not work today. |
| he/she | उहाँ आज काम गर्दिनन्।<br>uhā̃ āja kām gardinan | She does not work today. |
| they | उहाँहरू आज काम गर्दैनन्।<br>uhā̃harū āja kām gardainan | They do not work today. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

An action negative is different from छैन for nonexistence. Check the respectful negative forms as well.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 10 Present progressive (~ing)

Describe an action in progress

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use a verb stem with दै/्दै + a form of छ: पढ्दै छु, I am reading; खाँदै छु, I am eating. Both गर्दै छु and गरिरहेको छु can express ongoing action. Negate the final auxiliary with the छैन family. जाँदै छु can describe going now or an imminent arrangement, depending on time expressions and context.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म अहिले काम गर्दैछु।<br>ma ahile kām gardaichu | I am working now. |
| we | हामी अहिले काम गर्दैछौं।<br>hāmī ahile kām gardaichaũ | We are working now. |
| you | तपाईं अहिले काम गर्दैहुनुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩ ahile kām gardaihunuhuncha | You are working now. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू अहिले काम गर्दैहुनुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩharū ahile kām gardaihunuhuncha | You are working now. |
| he/she | उहाँ अहिले काम गर्दैछिन्।<br>uhā̃ ahile kām gardaichin | She is working now. |
| they | उहाँहरू अहिले काम गर्दैछन्।<br>uhā̃harū ahile kām gardaichan | They are working now. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Distinguish an ongoing action from a state left by a completed action. Do not add ले merely because the action verb is transitive.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: Use a verb stem with दै/्दै + a form of छ: पढ्दै छु, I am reading; खाँदै छु, I am eating. Both गर्दै छु and गरिरहेको छु can express ongoing action. Negate the final auxiliary with the छैन family. जाँदै छु can describe going now or an imminent arrangement, depending on time expressions and context.

## 11 The ergative ले & the past (did ~)

Describe completed actions and the agent marker ले

### Meaning and sentence construction

Transitive verbs take an object, as in read a book or write a letter. Completed transitive events often mark the agent with मैले or उहाँले, and verb agreement also matters. Do not add ले to every past subject: go, come and sleep are intransitive. Learn irregular past stems such as गर्नु→गरेँ, जानु→गएँ and आउनु→आएँ.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | मैले हिजो काम गरेँ।<br>maile hijo kām garẽ | I worked yesterday. |
| we | हामीले हिजो काम गर्यौं।<br>hāmīle hijo kām garyaũ | We worked yesterday. |
| you | तपाईंले हिजो काम गर्नुभयो।<br>tapāĩle hijo kām garnubhayo | You worked yesterday. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरूले हिजो काम गर्नुभयो।<br>tapāĩharūle hijo kām garnubhayo | You worked yesterday. |
| he/she | उहाँले हिजो काम गरिन्।<br>uhā̃le hijo kām garin | She worked yesterday. |
| they | उहाँहरूले हिजो काम गरे।<br>uhā̃harūle hijo kām gare | They worked yesterday. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

### Past existence uses the थियो family

| Person | Past form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म अस्ति बजारमा थिएँ।<br>ma asti bajārmā thiẽ | I was at the market the day before yesterday. |
| we | हामी अस्ति बजारमा थियौं।<br>hāmī asti bajārmā thiyaũ | We were at the market the day before yesterday. |
| you | तपाईं अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>tapāĩ asti bajārmā hunuhunthyo | You were at the market the day before yesterday. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>tapāĩharū asti bajārmā hunuhunthyo | You were at the market the day before yesterday. |
| he/she | उहाँ अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>uhā̃ asti bajārmā hunuhunthyo | She was at the market the day before yesterday. |
| they | उहाँहरू अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>uhā̃harū asti bajārmā hunuhunthyo | They were at the market the day before yesterday. |

Do not mark an intransitive subject like the agent of a transitive action. Some verbs also change their stem in the past.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: Transitive verbs take an object, as in read a book or write a letter. Completed transitive events often mark the agent with मैले or उहाँले, and verb agreement also matters. Do not add ले to every past subject: go, come and sleep are intransitive. Learn irregular past stems such as गर्नु→गरेँ, जानु→गएँ and आउनु→आएँ.

| --- | --- | --- |

| बजार | bajār | market |
| गयौं | gayaũ | we went (past of जानु) |

How it works: Transitive verbs take an object, as in read a book or write a letter. Completed transitive events often mark the agent with मैले or उहाँले, and verb agreement also matters. Do not add ले to every past subject: go, come and sleep are intransitive. Learn irregular past stems such as गर्नु→गरेँ, जानु→गएँ and आउनु→आएँ.

Do not add ले to every past subject: go, come and sleep are intransitive. Learn irregular past stems such as गर्नु→गरेँ, जानु→गएँ and आउनु→आएँ.

## 12 Past negative (didn't ~)

Say what did not happen in the past

### Meaning and sentence construction

Past negatives such as गरेनँ and गएनँ differ from present गर्दिनँ. Choose person/respect forms such as गरेनँ, गरेनन् and गर्नुभएन. Check agent marking even in a negated completed transitive event. Past nonexistence with थिएन is a separate copula construction.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | मैले हिजो काम गरिनँ।<br>maile hijo kām garinã | I did not work yesterday. |
| we | हामीले हिजो काम गरेनौं।<br>hāmīle hijo kām garenaũ | We did not work yesterday. |
| you | तपाईंले हिजो काम गर्नुभएन।<br>tapāĩle hijo kām garnubhaena | You did not work yesterday. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरूले हिजो काम गर्नुभएन।<br>tapāĩharūle hijo kām garnubhaena | You did not work yesterday. |
| he/she | उहाँले हिजो काम गरिनन्।<br>uhā̃le hijo kām garinan | She did not work yesterday. |
| they | उहाँहरूले हिजो काम गरेनन्।<br>uhā̃harūle hijo kām garenan | They did not work yesterday. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

### Past existence uses the थियो family

| Person | Past form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म अस्ति बजारमा थिइनँ।<br>ma asti bajārmā thiinã | I was not at the market the day before yesterday. |
| we | हामी अस्ति बजारमा थिएनौं।<br>hāmī asti bajārmā thienaũ | We were not at the market the day before yesterday. |
| you | तपाईं अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्नथ्यो।<br>tapāĩ asti bajārmā hunuhunnathyo | You were not at the market the day before yesterday. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्नथ्यो।<br>tapāĩharū asti bajārmā hunuhunnathyo | You were not at the market the day before yesterday. |
| he/she | उहाँ अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्नथ्यो।<br>uhā̃ asti bajārmā hunuhunnathyo | She was not at the market the day before yesterday. |
| they | उहाँहरू अस्ति बजारमा हुनुहुन्नथ्यो।<br>uhā̃harū asti bajārmā hunuhunnathyo | They were not at the market the day before yesterday. |

The agent keeps ले in a negative completed transitive sentence. This differs from saying something has not happened yet.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 13 Future (will ~)

Talk about future actions and plans

### Meaning and sentence construction

Future forms include गर्नेछु and जानेछु: a ने stem with personal छ-family endings. A respectful form is गर्नुहुनेछ. Time words such as भोलि can also allow a present form to describe a plan. Distinguish the dedicated future form from other ways of expressing a future arrangement.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म भोलि काम गर्छु।<br>ma bholi kām garchu | I will work tomorrow. |
| we | हामी भोलि काम गर्छौं।<br>hāmī bholi kām garchaũ | We will work tomorrow. |
| you | तपाईं भोलि काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩ bholi kām garnuhuncha | You will work tomorrow. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू भोलि काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>tapāĩharū bholi kām garnuhuncha | You will work tomorrow. |
| he/she | उहाँ भोलि काम गर्नुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃ bholi kām garnuhuncha | She will work tomorrow. |
| they | उहाँहरू भोलि काम गर्छन्।<br>uhā̃harū bholi kām garchan | They will work tomorrow. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

A definite plan and an uncertain prediction are different. Practice ways to soften predictions in the inference lesson.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 14 Habitual past (used to ~)

Describe former habits

### Meaning and sentence construction

Forms such as गर्थें and बस्थें describe former habits. Contrast a single completed गरेँ with habitual गर्थें. Negatives include गर्दिनथें; respectful forms include गर्नुहुन्थ्यो. पहिले means formerly, and सानो छँदा means when young.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म पहिले यहाँ काम गर्थें।<br>ma pahile yahā̃ kām garthẽ | I used to work here. |
| we | हामी पहिले यहाँ काम गर्थ्यौं।<br>hāmī pahile yahā̃ kām garthyaũ | We used to work here. |
| you | तपाईं पहिले यहाँ काम गर्नुहुन्थ्यो।<br>tapāĩ pahile yahā̃ kām garnuhunthyo | You used to work here. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू पहिले यहाँ काम गर्नुहुन्थ्यो।<br>tapāĩharū pahile yahā̃ kām garnuhunthyo | You used to work here. |
| he/she | उहाँ पहिले यहाँ काम गर्थिन्।<br>uhā̃ pahile yahā̃ kām garthin | She used to work here. |
| they | उहाँहरू पहिले यहाँ काम गर्थे।<br>uhā̃harū pahile yahā̃ kām garthe | They used to work here. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Do not automatically copy the ले marking of a single completed transitive event into a habitual past pattern.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 15 Present perfect / experience (have done ~)

Describe experience and present results

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use -एको/-एकी/-एका with the छ family. गरेको छु can describe a current result; खाएको छु can express experience in context. Distinguish these participial endings from unrelated question forms such as केको. Negate the auxiliary with the छैन family and use words such as अझै, still. थाकेको छु describes a resulting state: I am tired.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | मैले यो काम गरेको छु।<br>maile yo kām gareko chu | I have done this work. |
| we | हामीले यो काम गरेको छौं।<br>hāmīle yo kām gareko chaũ | We have done this work. |
| you | तपाईंले यो काम गर्नुभएको छ।<br>tapāĩle yo kām garnubhaeko cha | You have done this work. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरूले यो काम गर्नुभएको छ।<br>tapāĩharūle yo kām garnubhaeko cha | You have done this work. |
| he/she | उहाँले यो काम गरेकी हुनुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃le yo kām garekī hunuhuncha | She has done this work. |
| they | उहाँहरूले यो काम गरेका हुनुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃harūle yo kām garekā hunuhuncha | They have done this work. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Context distinguishes experience, completion and a resulting state. A negative can express not yet.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 16 Past perfect (had done ~)

Describe something completed before a past reference point

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use -एको and related forms with the थियो family for a result already established at a past time. गरेको थिएँ means I had done it. Compare present-perfect छ with past-reference थियो forms. Negatives include थिइनँ and थिएन. Identify the reference event and which event happened first.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | मैले यो काम गरेको थिएँ।<br>maile yo kām gareko thiẽ | I had done this work. |
| we | हामीले यो काम गरेको थियौं।<br>hāmīle yo kām gareko thiyaũ | We had done this work. |
| you | तपाईंले यो काम गर्नुभएको थियो।<br>tapāĩle yo kām garnubhaeko thiyo | You had done this work. |
| you plural | तपाईंहरूले यो काम गर्नुभएको थियो।<br>tapāĩharūle yo kām garnubhaeko thiyo | You had done this work. |
| he/she | उहाँले यो काम गरेकी हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>uhā̃le yo kām garekī hunuhunthyo | She had done this work. |
| they | उहाँहरूले यो काम गरेका हुनुहुन्थ्यो।<br>uhā̃harūle yo kām garekā hunuhunthyo | They had done this work. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Distinguish an action that was in progress then from one already completed then. This pattern combines a past participle with a past copula.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 17 Requests & commands (three politeness levels)

Make requests at an appropriate level of respect

### Meaning and sentence construction

Start with respectful -नुहोस्: बस्नुहोस्, please sit; लेख्नुहोस्, please write. Conversational variants such as बस्नुस् also occur. Keep familiar imperatives distinct from respectful ones. कृपया adds please; it does not replace the verb ending.

A short form can sound forceful with the wrong person. Small particles after a request can also change its tone.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: Start with respectful -नुहोस्: बस्नुहोस्, please sit; लेख्नुहोस्, please write. Conversational variants such as बस्नुस् also occur. Keep familiar imperatives distinct from respectful ones. कृपया adds please; it does not replace the verb ending.

## 18 Hortative / volitional (let's ~ / shall I ~)

Suggest a shared action and express intention

### Meaning and sentence construction

Suggestions include जाऔं, गरौं and बसौं. हामी जाऔं means let us go. A first-person offer or question can use म जाऊँ? Distinguish these from ordinary present statements. Negative suggestions prefix न, as in नजाऔं.

Shall I and let us use different persons. A suggestion not to do something places न before the verb.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 19 Prohibition (must not ~ / don't ~)

Express negative requests and prohibition

### Meaning and sentence construction

A polite negative request uses न + request form: नआउनुहोस्, please do not come. A general prohibition uses the -न infinitive + हुँदैन: यहाँ बस्न हुँदैन, sitting here is not allowed. Distinguish a prohibition from a statement that someone does not do something.

Keep a negative request separate from a prohibition with हुँदैन. Neither is the same as saying an action is unnecessary.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: A polite negative request uses न + request form: नआउनुहोस्, please do not come. A general prohibition uses the -न infinitive + हुँदैन: यहाँ बस्न हुँदैन, sitting here is not allowed. Distinguish a prohibition from a statement that someone does not do something.

| यहाँ | yahā̃ | here |
| बस्नु | basnu | sit; stay |
| हुँदैन | hũdaina | must not |

How it works: A polite negative request uses न + request form: नआउनुहोस्, please do not come. A general prohibition uses the -न infinitive + हुँदैन: यहाँ बस्न हुँदैन, sitting here is not allowed. Distinguish a prohibition from a statement that someone does not do something.
This is a question. Supply the missing information and keep person agreement on the final verb.

## 20 Ability (can ~)

Express ability and inability

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use the action infinitive in -न + a form of सक्नु. बोल्न सक्छु means I can speak; चलाउन सक्दिनँ means I cannot drive. Negation and personal/respect agreement appear on सक्नु, not on both verbs.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म नेपाली बोल्न सक्छु।<br>ma nepālī bolna sakchu | I can speak Nepali. |
| we | हामी नेपाली बोल्न सक्छौं।<br>hāmī nepālī bolna sakchaũ | We can speak Nepali. |
| you | तपाईं नेपाली बोल्न सक्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ nepālī bolna saknuhuncha? | Can you speak Nepali? |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू नेपाली बोल्न सक्नुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩharū nepālī bolna saknuhuncha? | Can you speak Nepali? |
| he/she | उहाँ नेपाली बोल्न सक्नुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃ nepālī bolna saknuhuncha | She can speak Nepali. |
| they | उहाँहरू नेपाली बोल्न सक्नुहुन्छ।<br>uhā̃harū nepālī bolna saknuhuncha | They can speak Nepali. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

The action's ordinary present form differs from a can construction. Negate सक्नु to say cannot.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 21 Obligation & permission (must ~ / may ~)

Distinguish obligation, absence of obligation and permission

### Meaning and sentence construction

-नु पर्छ expresses obligation; -नु पर्दैन means it is unnecessary, not forbidden. -न मिल्छ? asks whether an action is permitted or feasible. Some constructions use an experiencer such as मलाई; distinguish that role from the object.

Do not have to does not mean must not. In this obligation pattern, पर्छ does not change for the subject's person.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

How it works: -नु पर्छ expresses obligation; -नु पर्दैन means it is unnecessary, not forbidden. -न मिल्छ? asks whether an action is permitted or feasible. Some constructions use an experiencer such as मलाई; distinguish that role from the object.

## 22 Desire (want to ~)

Express wishes and what you feel like doing

### Meaning and sentence construction

-न चाहनु expresses a wish. An experiencer + -न मन लाग्नु expresses feeling like doing something. In मलाई चिया पिउन मन लाग्छ, मलाई marks the experiencer, पिउन is drink, and मन लाग्छ expresses inclination. Negation and tense appear mainly in the final verb.

### A reference table for comparing personal forms

| Person | Form and reading | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| I | म नेपाल जान चाहन्छु।<br>ma nepāl jāna cāhanchu | I want to go to Nepal. |
| we | हामी नेपाल जान चाहन्छौं।<br>hāmī nepāl jāna cāhanchaũ | We want to go to Nepal. |
| you | तपाईं नेपाल जान चाहनुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩ nepāl jāna cāhanuhuncha? | Do you want to go to Nepal? |
| you plural | तपाईंहरू नेपाल जान चाहनुहुन्छ?<br>tapāĩharū nepāl jāna cāhanuhuncha? | Do you want to go to Nepal? |
| he/she | उहाँ नेपाल जान चाहन्छिन्।<br>uhā̃ nepāl jāna cāhanchin | She wants to go to Nepal. |
| they | उहाँहरू नेपाल जान चाहँदैनन्।<br>uhā̃harū nepāl jāna cāhãdainan | They do not want to go to Nepal. |

Repetition in this table is for comparing agreement. The practice below varies situations and content. See appendices A/B for fuller forms and topic 7 for respect.

Keep liking something separate from wanting to do it. Distinguish मन पर्छ from मन छ and मन लाग्छ.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: -न चाहनु expresses a wish. An experiencer + -न मन लाग्नु expresses feeling like doing something. In मलाई चिया पिउन मन लाग्छ, मलाई marks the experiencer, पिउन is drink, and मन लाग्छ expresses inclination. Negation and tense appear mainly in the final verb.

उहाँलाई अहिले बोल्न मन छैन।
uhā̃lāī ahile bolna man chaina

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| उहाँलाई | uhā̃lāī | to him/her; him/her as object (respectful) |
| अहिले | ahile | now |
| बोल्न | bolna | speak |
| मन | man | mind; desire |
| छैन | chaina | not be; not exist |

How it works: -न चाहनु expresses a wish. An experiencer + -न मन लाग्नु expresses feeling like doing something. In मलाई चिया पिउन मन लाग्छ, मलाई marks the experiencer, पिउन is drink, and मन लाग्छ expresses inclination. Negation and tense appear mainly in the final verb.

| बस्न | basna | sit; stay |

सँग = company/participant or possession

## 23 Adjective agreement & position

Choose adjective position and agreement

### Meaning and sentence construction

Adjectives normally precede nouns. Some change form, as in राम्रो, राम्री and राम्रा; others such as सुन्दर are normally invariable. Do not assign gender to objects by Japanese or English intuitions. Review genitive को/की/का agreement with topic 5.

Not every adjective changes in the same way. Learn feminine, plural and respectful patterns with actual nouns.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

How it works: Adjectives normally precede nouns. Some change form, as in राम्रो, राम्री and राम्रा; others such as सुन्दर are normally invariable. Do not assign gender to objects by Japanese or English intuitions. Review genitive को/की/का agreement with topic 5.

## 24 Adverbs & exclamations

Add manner, frequency and degree

### Meaning and sentence construction

बिस्तारै means slowly, छिटो quickly/early, सधैं always, प्रायः usually, and धेरै very/much. Distinguish words describing an action from adjectives describing nouns. कति + adjective can express an exclamation as well as a question.

कति can be used for an exclamation as well as a question. Consider the purpose of the sentence, not just its punctuation.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

How it works: बिस्तारै means slowly, छिटो quickly/early, सधैं always, प्रायः usually, and धेरै very/much. Distinguish words describing an action from adjectives describing nouns. कति + adjective can express an exclamation as well as a question.

## 25 Comparison & superlative

Compare things and express similarity

### Meaning and sentence construction

A + Bभन्दा + adjective compares A against B. सबभन्दा/सबैभन्दा express a superlative; मध्ये identifies the group selected from. जस्तै/जस्तो and related forms express similarity; जस्तो can agree with its noun. Keep the item and comparison standard distinct.

### Related forms taught in this topic

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| भन्दा<br>bhandā | than | चियाभन्दा / ciyābhandā |
| मध्ये<br>madhye | among a group | यीमध्ये / yīmadhye |
| जस्तै・जस्तो・झैं・सरी<br>jastai / jasto / jhaĩ / sarī | like, similar to; जस्तो may agree | मेरो झोलाजस्तै / mero jholājastai |

The best or biggest should have a clear comparison group. Do not add an English-style comparative ending to the adjective.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

This house is bigger than that house.
यो घर त्यो घरभन्दा ठूलो छ।
yo ghar tyo gharbhandā ṭhūlo cha

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| यो | yo | this |
| घर | ghar | home; house |
| त्यो | tyo | that |
| घरभन्दा | gharbhandā | than that house |
| ठूलो | ṭhūlo | big |
| छ | cha | am; is; are |

How it works: A + Bभन्दा + adjective compares A against B. सबभन्दा/सबैभन्दा express a superlative; मध्ये identifies the group selected from. जस्तै/जस्तो and related forms express similarity; जस्तो can agree with its noun. Keep the item and comparison standard distinct.
भन्दा = standard of comparison

### Exercise 2

This book is cheaper than that book.

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| यो | yo | this |
| किताब | kitāb | book |
| त्यो | tyo | that |
| किताबभन्दा | kitābbhandā | than that book |
| सस्तो | sasto | cheap |
| छ | cha | is; are (existence) |

How it works: A + Bभन्दा + adjective compares A against B. सबभन्दा/सबैभन्दा express a superlative; मध्ये identifies the group selected from. जस्तै/जस्तो and related forms express similarity; जस्तो can agree with its noun. Keep the item and comparison standard distinct.
भन्दा = standard of comparison

## 26 Conjunctions (and / so / but)

Connect addition, sequence, contrast and reason

### Meaning and sentence construction

र joins items; अनि marks sequence; तर marks contrast; पनि adds also. किनभने introduces a reason, and forms such as -एकोले create reason clauses. Distinguish cause phrases such as का कारण from concession with का बाबजुद. A reason is a circumstance; a purpose is an intended outcome.

### Related forms taught in this topic

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| र・अनि・तर・पनि<br>ra / ani / tara / pani | and, then, but, also | चिया र पानी / ciyā ra pānī |
| किनभने・-एकोले<br>kinabhane / -ekole | because, since | समय नभएकोले / samay nabhaekole |
| का कारण・को कारणले・को वजहले<br>kā kāraṇ / ko kāraṇle / ko vajahle | because of | वर्षाका कारण / varṣākā kāraṇ |
| का बाबजुद・हुँदाहुँदै<br>kā bābajud / hũdāhũdai | despite; the latter is verbal | वर्षाका बाबजुद / varṣākā bābajud |

Confusing but with because changes the relationship between events. With पनि, also check what the word too applies to.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 27 While ~ (-दै) / after ~ (-एपछि)

Express simultaneity, sequence and deadlines

### Meaning and sentence construction

-दै expresses simultaneous actions; -एपछि orders an action after another. Noun + पछि means after an event such as class; it is not formed by simply adding पछि to any past finite verb. अघि/पहिले mean before, temporal भित्र means within, and पश्चात् is a more formal after. See topic 29 for जब/जहिले.

### Related forms taught in this topic

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| -दै・-एपछि<br>-dai / -epachi | while doing, after doing | पढ्दै / paḍhdai; पढेपछि / paḍhepachi |
| अघि・अगाडि・पहिले<br>aghi / agāḍi / pahile | before in time | खानुअघि / khānuaghi |
| पछि・पश्चात्<br>pachi / paścāt | after; पश्चात् is more formal | कक्षापछि / kakṣāpachi |
| भित्र<br>bhitra | within a period | दुई दिनभित्र / duī dinbhitra |

Translating a sequence as while changes the action. Begin with two actions performed by the same subject.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

Noun + पछि means after an event such as class; it is not formed by simply adding पछि to any past finite verb. अघि/पहिले mean before, temporal भित्र means within, and पश्चात् is a more formal after. See topic 29 for जब/जहिले.

| गर्नुहुन्छ | garnuhuncha | do/does (respectful) |

How it works: -दै expresses simultaneous actions; -एपछि orders an action after another. Noun + पछि means after an event such as class; it is not formed by simply adding पछि to any past finite verb. अघि/पहिले mean before, temporal भित्र means within, and पश्चात् is a more formal after. See topic 29 for जब/जहिले.

## 28 In order to ~ (-नको लागि)

Express a beneficiary or an intended action

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use को/का लागि for beneficiaries and -नको लागि for an intended action: छोराको लागि, for a son; पढ्नको लागि, in order to study. A motion purpose can also use a bare infinitive, as in पढ्न जान्छु. Learn synonymous निम्ति/निमित्त in actual combinations.

### Related forms taught in this topic

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| को लागि・का लागि<br>ko lāgi / kā lāgi | for a beneficiary; learn the construction | तपाईंका लागि / tapāĩkā lāgi |
| -नको लागि<br>-nako lāgi | in order to do | पढ्नको लागि / paḍhnako lāgi |
| निम्ति・निमित्त<br>nimti / nimitta | for, for the purpose of; further forms | तपाईंको निम्ति / tapāĩko nimti |

Purpose and cause are different. Check whether you mean an intended action or an existing circumstance.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

## 29 Relative expressions: people, things, place and time

Link people, things, places and times

### Meaning and sentence construction

Nepali can modify a noun with a -ने/-एको clause, or link clauses with forms such as जो and जुन. जो commonly refers to a person, जुन selects an entity, and जे refers to content. जहाँ marks place, जता direction, जब/जहिले time, जसरी manner, and जति amount/degree. These link information rather than ask who or where.

### Noun-modifying clauses and relative–correlative clauses

Place a modifier before its noun: नेपाल जाने मान्छे, a person who goes to Nepal; हिजो आएको मान्छे, a person who came yesterday. In जो यहाँ बस्नुहुन्छ, उहाँ मेरो साथी हुनुहुन्छ, जो introduces a person and उहाँ resumes that person. A clause describes an event/state. Each clause uses the case marking and verb appropriate to its own role.

| Relative form and reading | Function | Short example |
| --- | --- | --- |
| जो…उहाँ<br>jo…uhā̃ | the person who…that person | जो यहाँ बस्नुहुन्छ, उहाँ मेरो साथी हुनुहुन्छ।<br>jo yahā̃ basnuhuncha, uhā̃ mero sāthī hunuhuncha<br>The person who lives here is my friend. |
| जुन…त्यो<br>jun…tyo | the entity which…that one | जुन किताब तपाईं पढ्नुहुन्छ, त्यो राम्रो छ।<br>jun kitāb tapāĩ paḍhnuhuncha, tyo rāmro cha<br>The book you are reading is good. |
| जे…त्यो／त्यही<br>je…tyo / tyahī | what…that, the very thing | जे मन पर्छ, त्यही खानुहोस्।<br>je man parcha, tyahī khānuhos<br>Eat what you like. |
| जसले…उहाँले<br>jasle…uhā̃le | the agent who…that agent | जसले मेहनत गर्नुहुन्छ, उहाँले सिक्नुहुन्छ।<br>jasle mehanat garnuhuncha, uhā̃le siknuhuncha<br>The person who makes an effort learns. |
| जसलाई…उहाँलाई<br>jaslāī…uhā̃lāī | the person whom/to whom…that person | जसलाई पानी चाहिन्छ, उहाँलाई दिनुहोस्।<br>jaslāī pānī cāhincha, uhā̃lāī dinuhos<br>Give water to the person who needs it. |
| जसको・जसकी・जसका<br>jasko / jaskī / jaskā | whose; agreement follows the possessed noun | जसको किताब हुनुहुन्छ, उहाँलाई दिनुहोस्।<br>jasko kitāb hunuhuncha, uhā̃lāī dinuhos<br>Give it to the person whose book it is. |
| जसमा・जुन…मा<br>jasmā / jun…mā | in which; choose according to the referent | जुन झोलामा किताब छ, त्यो मेरो हो।<br>jun jholāmā kitāb cha, tyo mero ho<br>The bag that has a book in it is mine. |
| जहाँ…त्यहाँ<br>jahā̃…tyahā̃ | where…there | जहाँ पानी छ, त्यहाँ जीवन छ।<br>jahā̃ pānī cha, tyahā̃ jīvan cha<br>Where there is water, there is life. |
| जहाँबाट…त्यहाँबाट<br>jahā̃bāṭa…tyahā̃bāṭa | from where…from there | जहाँबाट आउनुभयो, त्यहाँबाट फेरि आउनुहोस्।<br>jahā̃bāṭa āunubhayo, tyahā̃bāṭa pheri āunuhos<br>Come again from the place you came from. |
| जता…त्यता／त्यतै<br>jatā…tyatā / tyatai | in whichever direction…that way | जता उहाँ जानुहुन्छ, म पनि त्यतै जान्छु।<br>jatā uhā̃ jānuhuncha, ma pani tyatai jānchu<br>I go in the same direction as she does. |
| जब…तब<br>jab…tab | when…then | जब पानी पर्छ, तब बाटो हिलो हुन्छ।<br>jab pānī parcha, tab bāṭo hilo huncha<br>When it rains, the road gets muddy. |
| जहिले<br>jahile | when, whenever, according to context | म जहिले आउँछु, उहाँ यहाँ हुनुहुन्छ।<br>ma jahile āũchu, uhā̃ yahā̃ hunuhuncha<br>Whenever I come, she is here. |
| जसरी…त्यसरी<br>jasarī…tyasarī | in the way…in that way | जसरी म गर्छु, त्यसरी गर्नुहोस्।<br>jasarī ma garchu, tyasarī garnuhos<br>Do it the way I do. |
| जसो…त्यसो<br>jaso…tyaso | as things are…accordingly | जसो भन्नुहुन्छ, त्यसो गर्छु।<br>jaso bhannuhuncha, tyaso garchu<br>I will do as you say. |
| जति…त्यति<br>jati…tyati | as much/many…that much/many | जति चाहिन्छ, त्यति लिनुहोस्।<br>jati cāhincha, tyati linuhos<br>Take as much as you need. |
| जस्तो…त्यस्तो<br>jasto…tyasto | of whatever kind…of that kind | जस्तो चाहिन्छ, त्यस्तो छान्नुहोस्।<br>jasto cāhincha, tyasto chānnuhos<br>Choose the kind that you need. |
| जत्रो…त्यत्रो<br>jatro…tyatro | of whatever size…of that size | जत्रो चाहिन्छ, त्यत्रो लिनुहोस्।<br>jatro cāhincha, tyatro linuhos<br>Take the size that you need. |
| जोसुकै・जुनसुकै・जेसुकै<br>josukai / junsukai / jesukai | whoever, whichever, whatever; extension | जुनसुकै किताब लिनुहोस्।<br>junsukai kitāb linuhos<br>Take any book. |
| जहाँसुकै・जहिलेसुकै・जतिसुकै<br>jahā̃sukai / jahilesukai / jatisukai | wherever, whenever, however much; extension | जहिलेसुकै आउनुहोस्।<br>jahilesukai āunuhos<br>Come whenever you like. |

को/कहाँ/कहिले ask questions; जो/जहाँ/जब link information. Temporal जब…तब is not always interchangeable with conditional यदि…भने. जहिले can introduce recurring times and its use depends on context. See topic 23 for agreement of जस्तो/जत्रो.

Distinguish a finite present statement from a noun-modifying form. Adding बित्तिकै can express as soon as.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

### Exercise 1

The person who lives here is my friend.
यहाँ बस्ने मान्छे मेरो साथी हो।
yahā̃ basne mānche mero sāthī ho

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| यहाँ | yahā̃ | here |
| बस्ने | basne | for sitting; who lives |
| मान्छे | mānche | person |
| मेरो | mero | my |
| साथी | sāthī | friend |
| हो | ho | am; is; are |

How it works: Nepali can modify a noun with a -ने/-एको clause, or link clauses with forms such as जो and जुन. जो commonly refers to a person, जुन selects an entity, and जे refers to content. जहाँ marks place, जता direction, जब/जहिले time, जसरी manner, and जति amount/degree. These link information rather than ask who or where.

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| काठमाडौं | kāṭhmāḍaũ | Kathmandu |
| जाने | jāne | going; that goes |
| बस | bas | sit; please sit |
| कहाँ | kahā̃ | where |
| छ | cha | is; are (existence) |

How it works: Nepali can modify a noun with a -ने/-एको clause, or link clauses with forms such as जो and जुन. जो commonly refers to a person, जुन selects an entity, and जे refers to content. जहाँ marks place, जता direction, जब/जहिले time, जसरी manner, and जति amount/degree. These link information rather than ask who or where.
This is a question. Supply the missing information and keep person agreement on the final verb.

| खानुहोस् | khānuhos | eat; please eat |

How it works: Nepali can modify a noun with a -ने/-एको clause, or link clauses with forms such as जो and जुन. जो commonly refers to a person, जुन selects an entity, and जे refers to content. जहाँ marks place, जता direction, जब/जहिले time, जसरी manner, and जति amount/degree. These link information rather than ask who or where.
जे…त्यो／त्यही = what…that, the very thing

### Exercise 22

I go in the same direction as she does.
जता उहाँ जानुहुन्छ, म पनि त्यतै जान्छु।
jatā uhā̃ jānuhuncha, ma pani tyatai jānchu

| Word or form | Reading | Meaning in this sentence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| जता | jatā | in the direction that (relative) |
| उहाँ | uhā̃ | he/she (respectful) |
| जानुहुन्छ | jānuhuncha | go/goes (respectful) |
| म | ma | I |
| पनि | pani | also; too |
| त्यतै | tyatai | that way; in that direction |
| जान्छु | jānchu | go; goes |

How it works: Nepali can modify a noun with a -ने/-एको clause, or link clauses with forms such as जो and जुन. जो commonly refers to a person, जुन selects an entity, and जे refers to content. जहाँ marks place, जता direction, जब/जहिले time, जसरी manner, and जति amount/degree. These link information rather than ask who or where.
जता…त्यता／त्यतै = in whichever direction…that way

## 30 Conditionals (if ~)

State a condition and what follows

### Meaning and sentence construction

Use condition + भने + consequence. यदि explicitly adds if but is often optional. Distinguish a condition about a current state, छ भने, from one about an event, भयो भने. Temporal जब…तब is not automatically interchangeable with conditional भने.

भने can mark the condition even without यदि. Practice what happens when the condition is not met as well.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

## 31 Inference / opinion / possibility (I think / probably / might / must be)

Distinguish opinion, prediction and possibility

### Meaning and sentence construction

मलाई लाग्छ introduces an impression or opinion; भन्ने can introduce its content. होला expresses a supposition and हुन सक्छ a possibility. -एको हुनु पर्छ can express an inference, unlike obligatory -नु पर्छ. Preserve the degree of certainty rather than translating a guess as a fact.

पर्छ has different roles in obligation and deduction. Check whether the whole construction means must do or must be.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

### Exercises, answers and how they work

भोलि पानी पर्छ जस्तो लाग्छ।

## 32 Passive (be ~ed)

Describe an event from the affected participant

### Meaning and sentence construction

A passive foregrounds what is affected: a written letter or opened door. Learn forms such as लेखियो and खोलियो, distinct from ordinary intransitives. लेखिन्छ means is written generally; लेखिएन means was not written. द्वारा can mark an agent; मार्फत an intermediary. Impersonal धेरै हिँडियो is not a literal passive of an object.

### Related forms taught in this topic

| Form and reading | Meaning and use | Short construction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| द्वारा<br>dvārā | by an agent | शिक्षकद्वारा / śikṣakdvārā |
| मार्फत<br>mārphat | through an intermediary | साथीमार्फत / sāthīmārphat |

Do not interpret an impersonal expression such as having done a lot of walking as being forced to walk by someone.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.

How it works: A passive foregrounds what is affected: a written letter or opened door. Impersonal धेरै हिँडियो is not a literal passive of an object.

## 33 Causative (make someone ~)

Describe causing or arranging an action

### Meaning and sentence construction

A causative expresses causing or arranging an action. Learn pairs such as खानु→खुवाउनु and सिक्नु→सिकाउनु. Separate causer, person acting and object. -न लगाउनु is another causative construction. Review लाई and completed-event agent marking in topics 5 and 11.

Seeing and showing, or learning and teaching, are different. Learn causative verbs by meaning rather than adding an ending mechanically.

### Before attempting the exercises

The usual order is subject + object/place/time + verb. Nouns name entities, adjectives describe qualities, and verbs express actions or states. Person identifies who, tense when, and respect the social relationship. Check person/gender/number/respect agreement in the tables, topic 7 and appendices A/B.

Listen to the examples to check word order and expressions, then practice saying them aloud yourself.
